[1;63r[60;1H; (B[0;1m[34mhttps://framework.zend.com/manual/1.12/en/zend.session.global_session_management.html[39m(B[m[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Some options that are often useful for Omeka sites are included here.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; session.name[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Sets the name used for the Omeka session cookie.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; default: ""[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; If left blank, Omeka will automatically select a unique session name.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1Hsession.name = ""[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; session.saveHandler[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Determines how session data will be saved.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; default: no setting (uses the database for saving session data)[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Sessions are now stored in the database by default. To revert to the[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; older method of storing session data in the filesystem, uncomment the[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; following line.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; session.saveHandler = ""[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;;;;;;;;;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Theme ;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;;;;;;;;;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; theme.useInternalAssets[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Whether Omeka should use locally-stored asset files.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; default: false[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Omeka includes some asset files from external sources, such as Google by[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; default. Set this to true if the Omeka installation does not have[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; web access, and Omeka will instead serve local copies of these files.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1Htheme.useInternalAssets = false[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Background Scripts ;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; background.php.path[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Path to PHP-CLI for running background processes.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; default: ""[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; If left blank, Omeka will try to autodetect the right path. Set this[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; to override the autodetected PHP path.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1Hbackground.php.path = ""[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; jobs.dispatcher[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; How Omeka "jobs" will be executed.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; default: "Omeka_Job_Dispatcher_Adapter_Synchronous"[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; Newer Omeka features and plugins use this setting to determine how[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; long-running jobs will be run.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H;[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; The default setting should work for all installations, but may[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; time out for longer jobs. On systems where the older PHP background[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; processes worked, the BackgroundProcess adapter can be used instead[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H; of the Synchronous one.[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1Hjobs.dispatcher.default = "Omeka_Job_Dispatcher_Adapter_Synchronous"[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1Hjobs.dispatcher.longRunning = "Omeka_Job_Dispatcher_Adapter_BackgroundProcess"[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d
[1;63r[60;1H[?25l[?12l[?25h7[2;61r8[61d

Change the mode of each FILE to MODE.
With --reference, change the mode of each FILE to that of RFILE.

  -c, --changes          like verbose but report only when a change is made
  -f, --silent, --quiet  suppress most error messages
